import Script from 'next/script';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { CONSENT_KEY } from '@/lib/analytics';
const GA_ID='G-8BRPGVJPXY';
export default function AnalyticsConsent(){
 const [open,setOpen]=useState(false);const [accepted,setAccepted]=useState(false);
 useEffect(()=>{try{const c=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CONSENT_KEY)||'null');const valid=c&&Date.now()-c.at<180*86400000;setOpen(!valid);setAccepted(valid&&c.choice==='accepted');}catch{setOpen(true);} const show=()=>setOpen(true);window.addEventListener('open-cookie-settings',show);return()=>window.removeEventListener('open-cookie-settings',show);},[]);
 function choose(choice:'accepted'|'refused'){
  try{localStorage.setItem(CONSENT_KEY,JSON.stringify({choice,at:Date.now()}));}catch{ /* Navigation remains available without storage. */ }
  if(choice==='refused'){
   (window as unknown as Record<string,unknown>)[`ga-disable-${GA_ID}`]=true;
   window.gtag?.('consent','update',{analytics_storage:'denied'});
   document.cookie.split(';').forEach(item=>{const name=item.split('=')[0].trim();if(name.startsWith('_ga')){for(const domain of ['',location.hostname,'.'+location.hostname,'.cphotobooth.fr'])document.cookie=`${name}=; Max-Age=0; path=/;${domain?' domain='+domain+';':''}`;}});
  }else{(window as unknown as Record<string,unknown>)[`ga-disable-${GA_ID}`]=false;window.gtag?.('consent','update',{analytics_storage:'granted'});}
  setAccepted(choice==='accepted');setOpen(false);
 }
 return <>{accepted&&<><Script id="analytics-init" strategy="afterInteractive">{`window.dataLayer=window.dataLayer||[];window.gtag=function(){window.dataLayer.push(arguments)};gtag('consent','default',{analytics_storage:'granted',ad_storage:'denied',ad_user_data:'denied',ad_personalization:'denied'});gtag('js',new Date());gtag('config','${GA_ID}',{allow_google_signals:false,allow_ad_personalization_signals:false});`}</Script><Script src={`https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${GA_ID}`} strategy="afterInteractive" /></>}{open&&<aside className="privacy-panel" aria-label="Votre choix de mesure dâ€™audience"><h2 style={{fontSize:'1.15rem'}}>Mesure dâ€™audience</h2><p>Avec votre accord, Google Analytics nous aide Ã  comprendre les visites. === FILE: C:\Users\pierr\OneDrive - Université de Tours\Documents\Photobooth\Site\c-photobooth-site\src\components\Button.tsx ===
import Link from "next/link";

type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "dark" | "ghost";

export function Button({
  href,
  children,
  variant = "ghost",
  className = "",
}: {
  href: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const classes =
    variant === "primary"
      ? "btn btn-primary"
      : variant === "dark"
      ? "btn btn-dark"
      : "btn";

  return (
    <Link href={href} className={`${classes} ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </Link>
  );
}

import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';

import { siteData } from '@/data/siteData';
import { trackEvent } from '@/lib/analytics';
import './Header.css';

export default function Header() {
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const toggleMenu = () => {
    setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen);
  };

  const closeMenu = () => {
    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <header className="header">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="header-content">
          <Link href="/" className="logo" onClick={closeMenu}>
            <Image
              src="/images/logo.png"
              alt={siteData.businessName}
              width={50}
              height={50}
              priority
            />
            <span className="logo-text">{siteData.businessName}</span>
          </Link>

          <button
            className={`hamburger ${mobileMenuOpen ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={toggleMenu}
            aria-label="Menu"
            aria-expanded={mobileMenuOpen}
            aria-controls="navigation-principale"
          >
            <span></span>
            <span></span>
            <span></span>
          </button>

          <nav id="navigation-principale" aria-label="Navigation principale" className={`nav ${mobileMenuOpen ? 'active' : ''}`}>
            <Link href="/" onClick={closeMenu}>Accueil</Link>
            <Link href="/tarifs" onClick={closeMenu}>Tarifs</Link>
            <Link href="/galerie" onClick={closeMenu}>Galerie</Link><Link href="/locations-evenementielles" onClick={closeMenu}>Autres locations</Link>
            <Link href="/conseils" onClick={closeMenu}>Conseils</Link>
            <Link

              onClick={() => {
                closeMenu();
                trackEvent('click_cta_header');
              }}
            >
              Devis
            </Link>
          </nav>
        </div>
      </div>

export const CONSENT_KEY = 'cphotobooth_analytics_consent_v1';
export function hasAnalyticsConsent() {
  try { const value=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CONSENT_KEY)||'null'); return value?.choice==='accepted' && Date.now()-value.at<180*24*60*60*1000; } catch { return false; }
}
declare global { interface Window { gtag?: (...args: unknown[]) => void; dataLayer?: unknown[]; } }
export function trackEvent(eventName: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>) {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && hasAnalyticsConsent() && typeof window.gtag === 'function') window.gtag('event',eventName,params??{});
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\pierr\OneDrive - Université de Tours\Documents\Photobooth\Site\c-photobooth-site\src\lib\pricing.ts ===
import prices from '@/data/tarifs.json';
export { prices };
export type Period = 'weekend' | 'soiree';
export type Formula = 'digitale' | 'impression' | 'complete';
export const formatPrice = (amount: number) => `${amount.toLocaleString('fr-FR')} â‚¬`;
export const formulaNames: Record<Formula, string> = { digitale: 'Digitale', impression: 'Impression', complete: 'ComplÃ¨te' };
export const formulas: Formula[] = ['digitale', 'impression', 'complete'];
export function includedPrints(period: Period, formula: Formula): number {
  return prices.impressionsIncluses[period][formula];
}
export function contactLink(formula: Formula, period: Period = 'weekend') {
  return `/contact?formule=${formula}&duree=${period}`;
}
export function deliveryPrice(km: number, formula: Formula): number | null {
  if (km < 0 || !Number.isFinite(km) || km > 60) return null;
  if (formula === 'complete' && km <= prices.inclus.livraisonCompleteKm) return 0;
  if (km <= 20) return prices.livraison.jusqua20km;
  if (km <= 40) return prices.livraison.de20a40km;
  return prices.livraison.de40a60km;
}
export function estimatePrice(period: Period, formula: Formula, km: number | null, extra: 0 | 100 | 200, custom: boolean) {
  const delivery = km === null ? 0 : deliveryPrice(km, formula);
  if (delivery === null) return null;
  const prints = formula === 'digitale' ? 0 : extra === 100 ? prices.options.impressions100 : extra === 200 ? prices.options.impressions200 : 0;
  return prices[period][formula] + delivery + prints + (custom && formula !== 'complete' ? prices.options.personnalisation : 0);
}
export function plans(period: Period) {
  return formulas.map(id => ({
    id, name: `Formule ${formulaNames[id]}${period === 'soiree' ? ' SoirÃ©e' : ''}`,
    price: formatPrice(prices[period][id]),
    duration: period === 'weekend' ? 'Weekend ou 48h en semaine' : 'SoirÃ©e en semaine uniquement',
    popular: id === 'impression', includesDelivery: id === 'complete',
    features: [
      'Photobooth en bois fabriquÃ© en France',
      'Appareil photo intÃ©grÃ© et photos numÃ©riques illimitÃ©es',
      'Photos disponibles aprÃ¨s votre Ã©vÃ©nement',
      ...(id === 'digitale' ? ['Sans impressions papier'] : [`${includedPrints(period, id)} impressions papier 10 Ã— 15 cm`, 'Imprimante professionnelle incluse']),
      ...(id === 'complete' ? ['Cadre photo personnalisÃ© inclus', `Livraison incluse jusquâ€™Ã  ${prices.inclus.livraisonCompleteKm} km de Tours`] : ['Retrait Ã  Tours â€¢ livraison en option']),
      'Guide dâ€™installation et assistance tÃ©lÃ©phonique',
    ],
  }));
}

import { prices } from '@/lib/pricing';
import type { Metadata } from 'next';

interface SEOParams {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  canonical: string;
  ogImage?: string;
  noindex?: boolean;
}

export function generateMetadata(params: SEOParams): Metadata {
  const {
    title,
    description,
    canonical,
    ogImage = 'https://www.cphotobooth.fr/images/photobooth-vrai.webp',
    noindex = false,
  } = params;

  return {
    title,
    description,
    alternates: {
      canonical,
    },
    robots: noindex ? 'noindex,nofollow' : 'index,follow',
    openGraph: {
      title,
      description,
      url: canonical,
      type: 'website',
      locale: 'fr_FR',
      siteName: 'CPhotobooth',
      images: [
        {
          url: ogImage,
          width: 1024,
          height: 1024,
          alt: title,
        },
      ],
    },
    twitter: {
      card: 'summary_large_image',
      title,
      description,
      images: [ogImage],
    },
  };
}

export function generateLocalBusinessSchema() {
  return {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': ['LocalBusiness', 'ProfessionalService'],
    '@id': 'https://www.cphotobooth.fr/#organization',
    name: 'CPhotobooth',
    image: 'https://www.cphotobooth.fr/icon.png',
    logo: 'https://www.cphotobooth.fr/icon.png',
    url: 'https://www.cphotobooth.fr',
    telephone: '+33781415195',
    email: 'contact@cphotobooth.fr',
    priceRange: `${prices.soiree.digitale}â‚¬â€“${prices.weekend.complete}â‚¬`,
    areaServed: [
      {
        '@type': 'City',
        name: 'Tours',
      },
      { '@type': 'City', name: 'Blois' },
      { '@type': 'City', name: 'Amboise' },
      { '@type': 'AdministrativeArea', name: 'Loir-et-Cher' },
      {
        '@type': 'AdministrativeArea',
        name: 'Indre-et-Loire',
      },
      {
        '@type': 'AdministrativeArea',
        name: 'Centre-Val de Loire',
      },
    ],
    sameAs: [],

  };
}

export function generateServiceSchema(serviceName: string, serviceDescription: string, serviceType: string) {
  return {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'Service',
    name: serviceName,
    description: serviceDescription,
    serviceType,
    provider: {
      '@id': 'https://www.cphotobooth.fr/#organization',
    },
    areaServed: { '@type': 'AdministrativeArea', name: 'Indre-et-Loire' },
  };
}

export function generateFAQSchema(faqs: Array<{ question: string; answer: string }>) {
  return {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'FAQPage',
    mainEntity: faqs.map(faq => ({
      '@type': 'Question',
      name: faq.question,
      acceptedAnswer: {
        '@type': 'Answer',
        text: faq.answer,
      },
    })),
  };
}

export function generateBreadcrumbSchema(items: Array<{ name: string; url: string }>) {
  return {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'BreadcrumbList',
    itemListElement: items.map((item, index) => ({
      '@type': 'ListItem',
      position: index + 1,
      name: item.name,
      item: item.url,
    })),
  };
}

export function generateOfferSchema(offers: Array<{
  name: string;
  price: string;
  description: string;
}>) {
  return {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'OfferCatalog',
    name: 'Formules photobooth CPhotobooth',
    itemListElement: offers.map(offer => ({
      '@type': 'Offer',
      itemOffered: {
        '@type': 'Service',
        name: offer.name,
        description: offer.description,
      },
      price: offer.price.replace(/[^0-9,.]/g, '').replace(',', '.'),
      priceCurrency: 'EUR',
      url: 'https://www.cphotobooth.fr/tarifs',
    })),
  };
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\pierr\OneDrive - Université de Tours\Documents\Photobooth\Site\c-photobooth-site\next.config.js ===
/** @type {import('next').NextConfig} */

// Liste de toutes les villes (extraite de cities.ts)
const cities = require('./src/data/seo/cities.json').map(city => city.slug);

const nextConfig = {
  turbopack: { root: process.cwd() },
  images: {
    unoptimized: false,
    formats: ['image/webp'],
  },
  compiler: {
    removeConsole: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
  },
  compress: true,
  poweredByHeader: false,

  async redirects() {
    const cityRedirects = cities.map(city => ({
      source: `/photobooth/${city}`,
      destination: `/photobooth-${city}`,
      permanent: true,
    }));

    return [
      { source: '/photobooth-saint-georges-sur-cher', destination: '/photobooth-tours', permanent: true },
      { source: '/photobooth/saint-georges-sur-cher', destination: '/photobooth-tours', permanent: true },
      // Redirection permanente non-www â†’ www
      {
        source: '/:path*',
        has: [
          {
            type: 'host',
            value: 'cphotobooth.fr',
          },
        ],
        destination: 'https://www.cphotobooth.fr/:path*',
        permanent: true,
      },
      // Redirection /prix-photobooth-tours â†’ /tarifs
      {
        source: '/prix-photobooth-tours',
        destination: '/tarifs',
        permanent: true,
      },
      // Redirection /photobooth-anniversaire-tours â†’ /photobooth-tours
      {
        source: '/photobooth-anniversaire-tours',
        destination: '/photobooth-tours',
        permanent: true,
      },
      // Redirections /photobooth/:city â†’ /photobooth-:city
      ...cityRedirects,
    ];
  },

  async rewrites() {
    // Rewrites internes pour le rendu des pages villes
    // (le routing public passe par les redirections ci-dessus)
    const cityRewrites = cities.map(city => ({
      source: `/photobooth-${city}`,
      destination: `/photobooth/${city}`,
    }));

    return cityRewrites;
  },
};

module.exports = nextConfig;
